transition-colors inline-block" href="/help">← Back</a><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Plans and billing</h1>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Learn how plans, usage, and billing work with Grok Bot.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I get access to Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot access is included on every paid individual Cursor plan and on Cursor Teams. You can also grant usage to your Cursor account by linking an individual SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, SuperGrok Heavy, or X Premium+ subscription.</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Situation</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">What to do</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Grok Bot usage</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on <strong>Cursor Ultra</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Sign in to Grok Bot with the same account. Access is included.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Highest weekly usage</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on <strong>Cursor Pro+</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Sign in to Grok Bot with the same account. Access is included.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Generous weekly usage, below Ultra</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on <strong>Cursor Pro</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Sign in to Grok Bot with the same account. Access is included.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Weekly usage, below Pro+</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on a <strong>Cursor Teams</strong> plan (self-serve)</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Sign in with your Cursor account. Every member has access. No Premium seat or admin request required.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Follows the Teams plan allowance</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on <strong>Cursor Enterprise</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Consult your account executive to enable Grok Bot access for your team.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Managed by your admin</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You have <strong>individual SuperGrok Heavy</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Link your Grok account from the Grok Bot plan screen. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Highest linked usage</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You have <strong>individual SuperGrok Plus</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Link your Grok account from the Grok Bot plan screen. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Generous linked usage, below Heavy</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You have <strong>individual SuperGrok</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Link your Grok account from the Grok Bot plan screen. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Linked usage, below Plus</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You have <strong>X Premium+</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Link your X account from the Grok Bot plan screen. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Linked usage, below SuperGrok Plus</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You have <strong>SuperGrok Team</strong> or <strong>SuperGrok Enterprise</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">SuperGrok linking is not supported. Only individual SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, SuperGrok Heavy, or X Premium+ accounts can link.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Not included</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">You&#x27;re on <strong>SuperGrok Lite</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Grok Bot is not included. Upgrade to SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, or SuperGrok Heavy, then link again.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Not included</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Linking SuperGrok or X Premium+ is a <strong>usage grant</strong>, not a Cursor plan. Your existing Cursor plan stays in place after linking. You can still upgrade that Cursor plan, such as <strong>Pro+</strong> to <strong>Ultra</strong>. Linking SuperGrok on top of it does not add usage. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack">Do Cursor and SuperGrok plans stack?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="whats-the-difference-between-pro-pro-and-ultra-for-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#whats-the-difference-between-pro-pro-and-ultra-for-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="whats-the-difference-between-pro-pro-and-ultra-for-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to whats-the-difference-between-pro-pro-and-ultra-for-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What&#x27;s the difference between Pro, Pro+, and Ultra for Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Pro</strong> includes Grok Bot with weekly usage, below the Pro+ tier.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Pro+</strong> includes Grok Bot with generous weekly usage, below the Ultra tier.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Ultra</strong> includes Grok Bot with the highest weekly usage.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Pro, Pro+, and Ultra all sign in to Grok Bot the same way: use the same Cursor account. You can still upgrade within those Cursor plans. Moving from <strong>Pro+</strong> to <strong>Ultra</strong> raises included Grok Bot usage to the Ultra tier.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription" data-heading-base-id="do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription" aria-label="Copy link to do-i-need-a-separate-grok-bot-subscription"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Do I need a separate Grok Bot subscription?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. Access is included with Cursor Pro, Cursor Pro+, Cursor Ultra, or a Cursor Teams seat, or through an individual SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, SuperGrok Heavy, or X Premium+ account link.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="does-joining-a-cursor-teams-plan-require-a-premium-seat-for-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#does-joining-a-cursor-teams-plan-require-a-premium-seat-for-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="does-joining-a-cursor-teams-plan-require-a-premium-seat-for-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to does-joining-a-cursor-teams-plan-require-a-premium-seat-for-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Does joining a Cursor Teams plan require a Premium seat for Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. On a self-serve <strong>Cursor Teams</strong> plan, every member gets Grok Bot on their Cursor account. Team admins do not need to assign a Premium seat, and members do not need to request access.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot usage draws from the Teams plan&#x27;s usage allowance for that seat. On-demand usage is enabled by default for the Teams plan. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/account/teams/pricing#on-demand-usage">Team pricing</a> for seat allowances and on-demand details.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-does-grok-bot-usage-work"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-does-grok-bot-usage-work" data-heading-base-id="how-does-grok-bot-usage-work" aria-label="Copy link to how-does-grok-bot-usage-work"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How does Grok Bot usage work?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Paid Grok Bot access includes <strong>Weekly usage</strong>. That included grant resets weekly. When it runs out, extra Grok Bot usage can continue as <strong>On-demand usage</strong> if on-demand is enabled. That extra usage is billed through Cursor and counts toward the <strong>On-demand monthly limit</strong>. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage">What do the plan and spending screens call usage?</a> and <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot">How does the on-demand monthly limit apply to Grok Bot?</a>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot usage is metered on your Cursor account, not on Grok or X. A SuperGrok or X Premium+ link grants Grok Bot usage on that same Cursor account while SuperGrok or X Premium+ stays active. It does not create a second meter on Grok or X, and it does not change your Cursor plan. If you already have a Cursor plan that includes Grok Bot, the link does not add usage on top of that plan. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack">Do Cursor and SuperGrok plans stack?</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-does-the-grok-bot-free-trial-work-and-how-do-i-use-it-efficiently"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-does-the-grok-bot-free-trial-work-and-how-do-i-use-it-efficiently" data-heading-base-id="how-does-the-grok-bot-free-trial-work-and-how-do-i-use-it-efficiently" aria-label="Copy link to how-does-the-grok-bot-free-trial-work-and-how-do-i-use-it-efficiently"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How does the Grok Bot free trial work and how do I use it efficiently?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The Grok Bot free trial is a usage credit rather than a set number of days, though a 7-day window also applies.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The credit is drawn down by how much work your agents do, measured by agent steps and tokens rather than the number of messages you send. Larger or longer agent jobs use more, so how far the credit goes depends on how you use it.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To make your trial credit go further:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Start small and scope tasks tightly, then scale up once you see how much each task uses.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Check your usage on the plan screen before running large or long agent jobs.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="is-grok-bot-usage-shared-between-macos-and-ios"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#is-grok-bot-usage-shared-between-macos-and-ios" data-heading-base-id="is-grok-bot-usage-shared-between-macos-and-ios" aria-label="Copy link to is-grok-bot-usage-shared-between-macos-and-ios"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Is Grok Bot usage shared between macOS and iOS?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Yes. macOS and iOS share a single usage bucket tied to your signed-in Cursor account. Both apps call the same backend, so usage you see on Mac is the same quota shown on iOS.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-if-my-trial-ran-out-during-a-single-run"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-if-my-trial-ran-out-during-a-single-run" data-heading-base-id="what-if-my-trial-ran-out-during-a-single-run" aria-label="Copy link to what-if-my-trial-ran-out-during-a-single-run"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What if my trial ran out during a single run?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The trial is a usage credit, so a large or long-running agent task can use most or all of it at once. This is expected, and used trial credit is not restored or topped up.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To keep using Grok Bot, upgrade to <strong>Cursor Pro</strong>, <strong>Cursor Pro+</strong>, <strong>Cursor Ultra</strong>, or a <strong>Cursor Teams</strong> plan, or link an <strong>individual SuperGrok</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Plus</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Heavy</strong>, or <strong>X Premium+</strong> account.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-do-i-get-access-to-grok-bot">How do I get access to Grok Bot?</a>. To make the credit go further, see <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-does-the-grok-bot-free-trial-work-and-how-do-i-use-it-efficiently">How does the Grok Bot free trial work and how do I use it efficiently?</a>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If a run failed with an error rather than an unsatisfying result, report it by following <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/troubleshooting/reporting-bugs">Report a bug</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-happens-to-my-cursor-plan-when-i-link-supergrok-or-x-premium"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-happens-to-my-cursor-plan-when-i-link-supergrok-or-x-premium" data-heading-base-id="what-happens-to-my-cursor-plan-when-i-link-supergrok-or-x-premium" aria-label="Copy link to what-happens-to-my-cursor-plan-when-i-link-supergrok-or-x-premium"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What happens to my Cursor plan when I link SuperGrok or X Premium+?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Linking SuperGrok or X Premium+ is a usage grant, not a Cursor plan. It doesn&#x27;t change or cancel your Cursor plan.</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you already pay for <strong>Cursor Pro</strong>, <strong>Pro+</strong>, or <strong>Ultra</strong>, that subscription stays in place after linking.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you&#x27;re on a <strong>Cursor Teams</strong> seat, the seat stays in place after linking.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you had no Cursor plan before linking, you still don&#x27;t, and Grok Bot usage comes from the SuperGrok or X Premium+ grant alone.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A SuperGrok or X Premium+ link is permanent once created. You can&#x27;t unlink or move it to a different Cursor account, so sign in with the correct Cursor account before linking. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a> for the linking steps.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack" data-heading-base-id="do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack" aria-label="Copy link to do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Do Cursor and SuperGrok plans stack?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">No. A Cursor plan and a SuperGrok or X Premium+ link do not stack. They do not add extra Grok Bot usage on top of each other.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">You can still upgrade within Cursor plans. Moving from <strong>Pro+</strong> to <strong>Ultra</strong> raises included Grok Bot usage to the Ultra tier. That upgrade is a change of Cursor plan, not a second grant.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">What does not add usage is combining a Cursor plan with a SuperGrok or X Premium+ link:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you are on <strong>Pro+</strong>, linking <strong>SuperGrok Plus</strong> does not add Grok Bot usage.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you already have <strong>SuperGrok Heavy</strong> linked, purchasing <strong>Cursor Ultra</strong> does not add Grok Bot usage.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Linking <strong>SuperGrok</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Plus</strong>, <strong>SuperGrok Heavy</strong>, or <strong>X Premium+</strong> while you already have Grok Bot on Pro, Pro+, Ultra, or a self-serve Teams plan does not add usage.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">A Cursor plan does not add usage on top of an <strong>X Premium+</strong> link, and an <strong>X Premium+</strong> link does not add usage on top of a Cursor plan.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Your Cursor subscription and your SuperGrok or X Premium+ subscription can both stay active and keep billing on their own. Buying one does not cancel the other. Only the usage grants do not combine.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">When <strong>Weekly usage</strong> runs out, keep going with <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/overages">on-demand usage</a> if it is enabled on your Cursor account. On-demand is how you get more Grok Bot usage past the included grant. Linking SuperGrok or X Premium+ on top of a Cursor plan is not. If you subscribed in the iOS or Android app, enable on-demand on the web. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/on-demand-usage-for-mobile-subscriptions">On-demand usage for mobile subscriptions</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage" data-heading-base-id="what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage" aria-label="Copy link to what-do-the-plan-and-spending-screens-call-usage"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What do the plan and spending screens call usage?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot Settings and the Cursor dashboard use different names for the same two pools.</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Weekly usage</strong> in Grok Bot is included usage. It resets weekly. A SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, or SuperGrok Heavy title on that meter is the linked grant, not a second Cursor plan.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>On-demand usage</strong> in Grok Bot is extra usage after weekly usage runs out. The row says <strong>Billed through Cursor</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>On-demand monthly limit</strong> in Grok Bot is the cap on that extra usage. On the web, the same cap is <strong>Spending</strong> and <strong>Monthly Limit</strong>.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Included weekly usage is not the monthly limit. Using up weekly usage does not stop Grok Bot if on-demand is on.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-if-i-upgrade-supergrok-after-i-already-linked"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-if-i-upgrade-supergrok-after-i-already-linked" data-heading-base-id="what-if-i-upgrade-supergrok-after-i-already-linked" aria-label="Copy link to what-if-i-upgrade-supergrok-after-i-already-linked"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What if I upgrade SuperGrok after I already linked?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The grant follows the SuperGrok or X Premium+ plan on the linked account, but the Grok Bot screen can keep showing the old tier for a while.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If you upgrade SuperGrok to SuperGrok Plus or SuperGrok Heavy after linking, keep using Grok Bot. Allow up to 24 hours for <strong>Weekly usage</strong> to move to the new tier. You do not need to link again, and you cannot unlink to force a refresh.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Buying Cursor Ultra while you wait does not add usage. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#do-cursor-and-supergrok-plans-stack">Do Cursor and SuperGrok plans stack?</a>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If the old tier is still shown after 24 hours, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with the Cursor account email, the Grok account email, and a screenshot of <strong>Weekly usage</strong>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to how-does-the-on-demand-monthly-limit-apply-to-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How does the on-demand monthly limit apply to Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot draws from <strong>Weekly usage</strong> first. When that included grant runs out, extra Grok Bot usage continues on on-demand if it is enabled. That extra usage counts toward the <strong>On-demand monthly limit</strong> in Grok Bot, which is the same cap as <strong>Monthly Limit</strong> on the web.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To turn it on or change it:</p>
<ol class="mb-4 prose-ol-markers" style="list-style-type:decimal;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">In Grok Bot, open <strong>Settings</strong> and find <strong>On-demand monthly limit</strong>. Choose <strong>Enable</strong> if it is off.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If Grok Bot asks for a card, finish that on the web. In a browser, go to <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://cursor.com/dashboard">cursor.com/dashboard</a>, open <strong>Spending</strong>, and set <strong>Monthly Limit</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If you subscribed in the iOS or Android app, set that limit on the web. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/on-demand-usage-for-mobile-subscriptions">On-demand usage for mobile subscriptions</a>.</li>
</ol>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A limit is not a hard stop in the middle of a run. A bot already working can finish past the monthly limit. After that, on-demand stops until you raise the limit or the billing cycle resets. Leaving the limit unchanged does not add more included weekly usage.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If on-demand is off, Grok Bot stops when weekly usage runs out. The screen says you have reached your Grok Bot usage limit, and it resets with weekly usage.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="what-happens-if-i-downgrade-or-my-teams-seat-changes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#what-happens-if-i-downgrade-or-my-teams-seat-changes" data-heading-base-id="what-happens-if-i-downgrade-or-my-teams-seat-changes" aria-label="Copy link to what-happens-if-i-downgrade-or-my-teams-seat-changes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">What happens if I downgrade or my Teams seat changes?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A downgrade or a seat change changes which single grant Grok Bot uses. It does not stack with another plan.</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Downgrading SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, or SuperGrok Heavy lowers the linked grant after the same refresh window, up to 24 hours. Usage already spent this week still counts. If you are already past the new tier, further included usage stops. On-demand continues only if it is enabled.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Downgrading a Cursor plan, such as Ultra to Pro+, changes the Cursor weekly usage the same way. A SuperGrok link does not replace or add to that change.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">On a self-serve Teams plan, Grok Bot follows the seat you are on. A higher seat can raise weekly usage. A lower seat lowers it. You do not need a separate SuperGrok link, and a link does not add usage on top of the seat.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If an admin removes your Teams seat, the Teams grant ends. A SuperGrok or X Premium+ link already on that Cursor account does not turn into a larger grant, and it does not restore the Teams allowance.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Request-based Teams plans and Enterprise seats do not take a personal SuperGrok link. Those plans keep Grok Bot on the Cursor contract.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-cancel-my-grok-bot-trial"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-cancel-my-grok-bot-trial" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-cancel-my-grok-bot-trial" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-cancel-my-grok-bot-trial"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I cancel my Grok Bot trial?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">There is no charge to cancel, and the Grok Bot trial never becomes a paid plan.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Cancel Trial</strong> appears on free-plan accounts. On a paid plan or Teams seat, the trial ends on its own and there&#x27;s nothing to cancel.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Use <strong>Cancel Trial</strong> on the plan screen. That ends the trial right away, removes any remaining trial credit, and you can&#x27;t claim the trial again.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If you&#x27;re on a free plan and <strong>Cancel Trial</strong> doesn&#x27;t appear or the cancel doesn&#x27;t take effect, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-cancel-a-free-trial-i-started-in-the-ios-app"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-cancel-a-free-trial-i-started-in-the-ios-app" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-cancel-a-free-trial-i-started-in-the-ios-app" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-cancel-a-free-trial-i-started-in-the-ios-app"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I cancel a free trial I started in the iOS app?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">An App Store intro offer is different from the Grok Bot usage-credit trial. Apple runs it, and it turns into a paid Apple subscription when the trial ends unless you cancel first.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Cancel in <strong>Apple ID → Subscriptions</strong> before the trial ends. See Apple&#x27;s guide to <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://support.apple.com/guide/iphone/iph4e3e7324f/ios">viewing and canceling subscriptions</a>. For refunds on an Apple in-app charge, use <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://support.apple.com/billing">Apple&#x27;s Subscriptions and billing support</a>. Cursor can&#x27;t cancel or refund an Apple in-app trial or subscription for you.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/mobile-purchase#how-do-i-manage-or-cancel-a-mobile-subscription">How do I manage or cancel a mobile subscription?</a>. Android has no in-app trial.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-refunds-work-for-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-refunds-work-for-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="how-do-refunds-work-for-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-refunds-work-for-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do refunds work for Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot does not have a separate subscription, so it does not have a separate refund path.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Cursor plans.</strong> Paid access is billed through your Cursor plan (Pro, Pro+, or Ultra). Subscription refund eligibility follows <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/refunds">Refunds</a>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">These Cursor charges are not refundable:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">On-demand usage Grok Bot has already consumed</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Mid-cycle downgrades with usage (no prorated refund)</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Store purchases.</strong> Cursor can&#x27;t refund in-app store charges:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>iOS:</strong> <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://support.apple.com/billing">Apple&#x27;s Subscriptions and billing support</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Google Play:</strong> <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/2479637">Google Play&#x27;s refund support</a></li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>SuperGrok and X Premium+.</strong> Qualifying Grok accounts are individual SuperGrok, SuperGrok Plus, SuperGrok Heavy, and X Premium+. Those subscriptions are billed by xAI or X, not Cursor.</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>SuperGrok:</strong> see <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://docs.x.ai/grok/faq#can-i-get-a-refund-for-my-grok-or-supergrok-subscription">xAI&#x27;s FAQ</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>X Premium+:</strong> contact X for refunds</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="related"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#related" data-heading-base-id="related" aria-label="Copy link to related"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Related</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/supergrok">Link SuperGrok for Grok Bot</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/overages">Usage-based charges</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/mobile-purchase">Subscribe from the mobile app</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/account-and-billing/refunds">Refunds</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/sign-in">Sign in to Grok Bot</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/delete-account">Delete your Grok Bot account</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/account/teams/pricing">Team pricing</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">Get help</a></li>
</ul></div><section aria-labelledby="help-article-feedback-heading" class="not-prose border-border border-t pt-6"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3"><h2 class="font-medium text-foreground
